10a1(1): @#/ O /dri za rnams dang /mi 'am ci rnams dang /lto 'phye chen po rnams dang /gnod sbyin rnams dang /srin po rnams dang

10b5(1): 'dir mi rnams dang skal ba mnyam bar skye'o//zhes bya ba'i bar du sbyar ro//de 'dir rgyal po'i pho brang dam/mdun na 'don gyi khyim du skye bar 'gyur te/

10b8(1): chos dang 'thun zhing snyoms par legs par phab ste/gnas so zhes bya ba'i bar du sbyar te/lha'i 'khor lo rin po che thob par gyur la/des na gling bzhi leg+sa

11b1(1): lus che zhing drang ba bsus tho ba ce na/smras pa/byang chub sems dpas ni/sngon gyi srid pa zhes bya ba nas rgyas par gyur te/srog chags 'byung ba
